- **kit-copilot-engineering-team.agent**: Copilot engineering orchestrator — decomposes complex, multi-step customization tasks into research, execution, creation, and review phases. Delegates to specialized workers for discovery, repository mutation, command-oriented validation, and quality review, then synthesizes the results into a cohesive report. Choose this for workspace audits, multi-artifact projects, or end-to-end customization workflows. Also handles single-mechanism requests — delegates to the researcher for mechanism classification when needed, then routes to the appropriate creation skill. - **koda**: The slop detector. Names the prior art behind any proposed design and kills anything recognisable. Use on every concept before it is built, and on every finished page before it ships. - **kodadjiyata**: Кодаджията — ревю на код за бъгове и уязвимости (Next.js/React/TS/Prisma, Express/EJS/SQLite). Използвай при „прегледай промените“, „намери бъга“, преди merge или продукция, при съмнение за инжекция, XSS, IDOR, CSRF, изтичане на тайни или лични данни, race условие, N+1 или уязвима зависимост. Различен от Качествения (поддръжаемост и рефактор), Разбивача (активна атака, червен екип) и Изпитателя (пише тестовете). Докладва с file:line, тежест, увереност и минимална поправка. ([stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few](https://github.com/stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kodadjiyata.md))
- **kodafraedingur**: Kóðafræðingurinn (Lisbeth Salander) — gætir þess að RÉTTA aðferðin sé notuð í kóðavinnu í Slökkvitæki-appinu: patch-mynstrið (IIFE, wrap, ?v= cache-bump), engin tvítök (variant-analysis), engin bein breyting á modal.js/features.js/pos.js nema rót-villa, próf (audit-all, vidmotsprof), switchView-race + _openCompanySafe, MutationObserver-bann, RLS-vitund. Les diff og segir RÉTT AÐFERÐ eða RÖNG AÐFERÐ. Kallaðu á hana áður en nýr patch er skrifaður; deploy bíða ekki eftir henni — hún fer yfir þau eftir á. Kveikjuorð: rétta aðferðin, kóðarýni, kóðayfirferð, yfirfara kóða, code review, diff, patch-… ([aggisigurds-dev/slokkvitaeki](https://github.com/aggisigurds-dev/slokkvitaeki/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/kodafraedingur.md))
